Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the markov_chains object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 116. The moduli space has dimension at most 542 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the markov_chains decomposition. By minimality, the element a^116 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 542-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
